TL;DR: 本文提出参数记忆定律（Parametric Memory Law），揭示LoRA微调中损失降低与有效参数、序列长度之间的幂律关系，并在token层面发现预测概率p>0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件。基于此提出MemFT阈值引导优化策略，动态将训练预算分配给低于阈值的token，提升记忆保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续学习/记忆更新研究（尤其LoRA）缺乏定量分析框架，对参数记忆的精确容量极限和动态过程理解不足。

Method: 1）提出参数记忆定律（幂律关系）；2）token级精细分析揭示确定性相变（p>0.5为逐字回忆充分条件）；3）提出MemFT阈值引导优化策略。

Result: 参数记忆定律（幂律）；概率相变阈值（p>0.5）；MemFT在记忆保真度和效率上的提升。

Conclusion: 通过量化框架和优化策略，为LLM参数化记忆的理解和增强提供了理论基础与实用方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>


### [112] [Loong: A Human-Like Long Document Translation Agent with Observe-and-Act Adaptive Context Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30274)
*Yutong Wang,Xuebo Liu,Derek F. Wong,Zhilin Li,Rongqing Jiang,Min Zhang,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Loong，一种基于3E记忆模块（Essence-Exemplar-Entity）和强化学习的长文档翻译智能体，通过自适应上下文选择机制显著提升翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在进行文档级翻译时面临两大挑战：有限的上下文窗口导致全局连贯性不足，以及冗余的上下文信息反而降低翻译质量。现有方法缺乏对历史信息的主动筛选和利用能力。

Method: 提出Loong智能体，包含三个核心组件：1) 3E记忆模块（摘要、句对、实体记录）存储历史上下文；2) 深度推理机制自适应识别最优上下文；3) 通过强化学习优化上下文选择策略，利用从自身采样轨迹中生成的偏好数据进行训练。

Result: 在英↔中、英↔德、英↔法三个翻译方向上，平均提升13.0分；展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力和上下文噪声鲁棒性；在超长文档翻译中保持显著稳定性。

Conclusion: Loong通过模拟人类翻译者的工作方式（选择性参考历史信息），有效解决了长文档翻译中的全局连贯性和上下文冗余问题，为文档级翻译提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Document-level translation remains one of the most challenging tasks for large language models, which are constrained by limited context windows that impede global cohesion, while simultaneously suffering from redundant contextual information that degrades translation quality. To address this, we propose a human-like long document translation agent called Loong, which leverages a 3E memory module (Essence-Exemplar-Entity) to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records as historical context. Instead of passively attending to all history, Loong performs deep reasoning to adaptively identify the optimal context for translation guidance. Loong optimizes its context policy through reinforcement learning, utilizing preference data derived from its own sampled observe-and-act reasoning trajectories. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Loong achieves substantial translation quality improvements in English $\Leftrightarrow$ Chinese, German, and French directions, with average gains of up to 13.0 points across the three evaluation metrics. Furthermore, Loong exhibits strong generalization across domains and robustness against contextual noise, while maintaining remarkable stability in ultra-long document translation. Our code is released at https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT.

</details>


### [113] [MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295)
*Valentina Bui Muti,Eugénie Dulout,Ziquan Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种从非结构化文本生成临床真实 HL7 FHIR R4 数据集的流水线，用于评估 LLM 在结构化临床数据上的诊断性能，发现 LLM 在 FHIR 结构化输入上的诊断准确率低于纯文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 临床评估基准多依赖静态数据集或非结构化输入，未反映临床系统中使用的结构化、互操作数据格式，缺乏在电子健康记录一致环境下的评估。

Method: 构建了结合分阶段 LLM 生成与术语验证修复的流水线，从非结构化文本生成 HL7 FHIR R4 数据，生成 MedCase-Structured 数据集，并进行诊断准确率对比实验。

Result: 82.5% 的案例生成了有效 FHIR 数据；LLM 在结构化 FHIR 输入上的诊断准确率始终低于纯文本输入。

Conclusion: 部署环境对齐的基准测试非常重要，LLM 在处理结构化临床数据时的表现差异凸显了实际临床部署中评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for clinical reasoning and decision support, but evaluation in realistic, electronic health record-congruent settings remains limited. Existing benchmarks often rely on static datasets or unstructured inputs that do not reflect the structured, interoperable data formats used in clinical systems. We introduce a pipeline for generating clinically realistic HL7 FHIR R4 bundles from unstructured text, enabling controllable evaluation of clinical decision support systems. The pipeline combines staged LLM generation with terminology-grounded validation and repair to reduce hallucinated codes and enforce structural and semantic consistency. Applying this approach to MedCaseReasoning, we construct MedCase-Structured, a synthetic dataset aligned with clinician-authored diagnostic cases, achieving valid FHIR generation for 82.5% of cases. Evaluation on MedCase-Structured reveals consistently lower diagnostic accuracy for LLMs on structured FHIR inputs than with plain text, highlighting the importance of deployment-aligned benchmarking.

</details>


### [114] [Resolution Diagnostics for Paired LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30315)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在公开LLM排行榜中，许多配对排名未达到常规配对检验的统计功效标准（如Open LLM Leaderboard v1中11/40、MMLU-Pro中4/9的相邻排名对未通过显著性水平0.05、功效0.8的检验）。作者将LLM配对评估形式化为假设检验问题，提出配对分辨率比率q=N/N*作为主要诊断指标，并发现广泛使用的非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)近似在接近比较时偏差约两倍，且多个主流计算器（Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr）在用户后乘(1-rho)时会继承这一偏差。该模式在多重校正和随时有效序贯检验下仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM排行榜的配对排名缺乏严格的统计检验支撑，许多排名差异可能不具统计显著性，导致排行榜可能误导用户对模型性能的判断。

Method: 将配对LLM评估形式化为假设检验问题，构建基于功效分析的分辨率比率q=N/N*诊断指标；推导小效应量下的二阶展开式，揭示非配对Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)近似的偏差；通过真实聚类和自举重采样验证稳定性。

Result: 11/40的Open LLM Leaderboard v1和4/9的MMLU-Pro前10相邻排名对在常规检验标准下不显著；非配对近似在接近比较时偏差约两倍；多个主流功效计算器继承此偏差；多重校正和序贯检验后不显著模式仍存在。

Conclusion: 当前LLM排行榜的许多排名缺乏统计显著性，现有简化近似方法（如Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho)）有系统性偏差，亟需采用正确的配对检验框架评估模型比较。

Abstract: Across two public LLM leaderboards, many displayed pairwise rankings do not meet a conventional paired-test resolution target under the actual paired evaluation design: 11 of 40 Open LLM Leaderboard v1 pairwise comparisons and 4 of 9 MMLU-Pro top-10 adjacent-rank pairs are unresolved at (alpha, 1-beta) = (0.05, 0.8). The MMLU-Pro count rises to 6/9 under real subject-level clustering and stays at 5-6 out of 9 in 99.9% of category-bootstrap resamples. We frame paired LLM evaluation as a hypothesis-testing problem, invert level-alpha, power-(1-beta) tests, and report a per-pair resolution ratio q = N/N* as the primary diagnostic. A sharp small-effect expansion with an explicit second-order constant shows that the widely-used unpaired Cohen-h-plus-(1-rho) shortcut deviates from the correct N* by approximately a factor of two in the close-comparison regime, a deficit that three of five off-the-shelf calculators(Cohen 1988, G*Power, R pwr) silently inherit when the user post-multiplies their per-arm output by (1-rho). The unresolved-pair pattern remains under multiplicity correction and anytime-valid sequential testing.

</details>


### [115] [COMPOSE: Composing Future Theorems from Citations and Formal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30333)
*David Busbib,Michael Werman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPOSE框架，通过结合科学引文图与形式定理依赖图，生成更合理、更数学化的未来定理声明，在arXiv-Mathlib数据集上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅建模科学引文或形式依赖中的单一约束，导致生成的数学主张要么缺乏动机（不遵循研究方向），要么缺乏基础（不遵循形式依赖）。作者希望同时利用两种互补上下文来生成更合理的未来数学定理。

Method: 提出COMPOSE，一个双图框架，条件化语言模型于科学引文上下文（来自arXiv引用图）和形式定理结构（来自Mathlib依赖图）。构建了包含108K配对科学-形式图样本的数据集和47K未来论文基准。

Result: COMPOSE在检索真实未来论文任务上超越强基线，在LLM评判评估下取得最佳整体性能，生成的输出更具基础性和数学丰富性。

Conclusion: 未来数学定理生成受益于结合科学语境与形式结构，双图方法能同时满足合理性约束和形式依赖约束。

Abstract: A plausible future mathematical claim must satisfy two constraints: it should follow the direction of prior work and respect the formal dependencies that constrain what can validly follow. Existing approaches typically model only one of these sources, producing claims that are either weakly grounded or insufficiently motivated. We introduce grounded future mathematical generation, where the goal is to generate a plausible future theorem-like claim for an anchor paper using two complementary sources of context: its scientific citation graph and aligned formal theorem dependency graph. To address this setting, we propose COMPOSE, a dual-graph framework that conditions a language model on both scientific citation context and formal theorem structure. To support this setting, we construct a dataset of 108K paired scientific-formal graph examples from arXiv and Mathlib, together with a benchmark of 47K future papers from 2024--2025. Experiments show that COMPOSE outperforms strong baselines on retrieval to real future papers and achieves the best overall performance under LLM-judge evaluation, producing more grounded and mathematically richer outputs. These results show that future mathematical generation benefits from combining scientific context with formal structure. Project page is available at https://david-busbib.github.io/COMPOSE-page/.

</details>


### [116] [Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343)
*Lukas Aichberger,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RiM（Reasoning in Memory）方法，用固定记忆块序列替代自回归生成推理步骤，实现大语言模型的潜在推理。记忆块作为工作记忆载体，可在单次前向传播中处理，通过两阶段课程学习（先监督预测推理步骤，后逐步丢弃步骤级监督并优化最终答案），在多个推理基准上匹配或超越现有潜在推理方法，同时避免自回归生成中间思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型通过自回归生成中间token来扩展测试时计算，这导致内部计算与外部通信耦合，不符合人类认知中利用工作记忆内部操纵信息而不外化中间思维的特点。

Method: RiM使用固定特殊token序列（记忆块）替代自回归生成的推理步骤，这些记忆块可在单次前向传播中处理。采用两阶段课程：第一阶段通过预测显式推理步骤来ground记忆块；第二阶段丢弃步骤级监督，在每个记忆块后迭代细化最终答案。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，RiM在不同系列和规模的语言模型中匹配或超越了现有潜在推理方法，同时避免了自回归生成思维token。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可以被训练使用工作记忆作为潜在推理的有效机制，RiM通过固定记忆块实现了计算高效的潜在推理。

Abstract: To improve the reasoning capabilities of large language models, test-time compute is typically scaled by generating intermediate tokens before the final answer. However, this couples reasoning to autoregressive generation and thereby conflates internal computation with external communication. In contrast, human cognition can use working memory to hold and manipulate information internally without the need to externalize intermediate thoughts. Drawing on this principle, we introduce Reasoning in Memory (RiM), a latent reasoning method that replaces the autoregressive generation of reasoning steps with memory blocks. These memory blocks are fixed sequences of special tokens that unlock the working-memory capacity of large language models. Since they are fixed rather than generated, they can be processed in a single forward pass, enabling compute-efficient latent reasoning. To operationalize these memory blocks, we employ a two-stage curriculum. First, we ground them by predicting explicit reasoning steps after each memory block. Second, we discard this step-level supervision and iteratively refine the final answer after each memory block. Our experiments on reasoning benchmarks show that, across language models of different families and sizes, RiM matches or exceeds existing latent reasoning methods while avoiding the autoregressive generation of thoughts. These results demonstrate that large language models can be trained to use working memory as an effective mechanism for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348)
*Yaxin Luo,Jiacheng Cui,Xiaohan Zhao,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Liu,Xinyue Bi,Zhaoyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出数据混合手术（DMS）框架，通过仅观察目标LLM生成的文本，反向估计其预训练语料的领域分布。作者设计了LLMSurgeon方法，利用标签偏移假设和校准的软混淆矩阵解决逆问题，并构建了LLMScan评估套件验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的预训练数据混合构成其"数字DNA"，但该组成极少公开，导致难以事后审计数据组合或来源。现有方法缺乏从生成文本反向推断预训练数据分布的能力。

Method: 将DMS形式化为标签偏移假设下的逆问题，提出LLMSurgeon框架：估计校准的软混淆矩阵，通过求解带约束的逆问题校正领域混淆并恢复潜在混合先验。

Result: 在LLMScan（基于开源LLM构建的可验证评估套件）上，LLMSurgeon在固定协议下以高保真度恢复领域混合分布。

Conclusion: 提供了一种无需访问训练数据即可事后审计基础模型数字DNA的实用方法，为LLM数据组成透明度提供了技术基础。

Abstract: The pretraining data mixture of Large Language Models (LLMs) constitutes their "digital DNA", shaping model behaviors, capabilities, and failure modes. Yet this composition is rarely disclosed, making post-hoc auditing of data combination or provenance difficult. In this work, we formalize $\textbf{Data Mixture Surgery (DMS)}$: given only generated text from a target LLM, estimate the domain-level distribution of its pretraining corpus under a predefined taxonomy. We propose $\textbf{LLMSurgeon}$, a strong framework that casts DMS as an inverse problem under the label-shift assumption. Rather than directly aggregating classifier outputs, LLMSurgeon estimates a calibrated $\textit{soft}$ confusion matrix and solves a constrained inverse problem to correct systematic domain confusion and recover the latent mixture prior. To evaluate, we introduce $\textbf{LLMScan}$, a recipe-verifiable evaluation suite built from open-source LLMs with transparent pretraining mixtures. Across LLMScan, LLMSurgeon recovers domain mixtures with high fidelity under fixed protocols. Our work presents a practical, post-hoc approach for auditing the digital DNA of foundation models without access to their training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STHTD-MP方法，用行为策略贝尔曼矩阵的对称部分替代协方差度量，并提供了完整的收敛性分析和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度时域差分方法在实际性能上强烈受辅助变量度量所诱导的几何结构影响，现有方法未充分利用行为策略转移信息来改进更新几何。

Method: 在原始-对偶鞍点公式中用行为策略贝尔曼矩阵的对称部分替代协方差度量，并对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤，保持原始变量和辅助变量单一学习率。

Result: 在标准随机逼近假设下提供了形式化收敛性分析；推导了投影预言机遍历间隙界和与GTD2-MP的精确均值算子比较；数值实验（二状态、Random Walk、Boyan Chain）支持所提条件；Baird反例被识别为奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: 当行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何时，STHTD-MP可取得比GTD2-MP更小的均值收缩因子，提供更有效的离策略预测更新几何。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [119] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了LLM生成的科学论文评审与人类评审的一致性，并发现作者可以利用LLM评审迭代修改论文以“刷分”，在特定场景下可使高达35%的论文获得统计显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的论文评审正被广泛使用甚至被大型会议官方试点，但LLM评审与人类评审的一致性尚不明确，且作者可能利用LLM辅助修改论文，需要系统评估这些影响。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文，从审稿人和作者两个角度进行实证实验：1) 比较LLM评审与人类评审的一致性；2) 分析不同提示和模型对一致性的影响；3) 模拟作者利用LLM评审迭代修改论文的“游戏”场景，评估其有效性。

Result: LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限，最佳情况下合理；一致性随提示和模型不同而变化；在特定场景下，作者通过迭代修改可显著提升评分，最多35%的论文获得统计显著提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审可被“刷分”利用，对学术评审的公正性构成潜在威胁，需要警惕并完善评审机制。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [120] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过将编辑型参数更新从加法形式重新表述为乘法形式的正交变换，解决了扩散模型中概念擦除与生成能力保持之间的权衡问题，实现了精确的概念擦除，并能在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编辑的概念擦除方法依赖加法参数更新，而加法更新会纠缠神经元的方向、幅度和角度几何，导致概念擦除与整体生成能力之间产生非预期的相互干扰。作者旨在解决这一核心限制。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）：(1) 从几何角度将编辑型擦除重新表述为乘法参数更新，应用层级的正交变换（源自参数的闭式解）；(2) 通过正交变换实现精确概念擦除，同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何不变；(3) 针对多概念擦除中的冲突约束，引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上，OCE在概念擦除效果和非目标保持方面均优于现有方法，能在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: OCE通过乘法正交变换替代加法更新，有效解耦了概念擦除与生成能力保持之间的冲突，是一种高效、可扩展且部署友好的概念擦除方法。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [121] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent是一个端到端多智能体系统，能够直接从输入图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析建模与仿真，通过多模态视觉-语言管道和优先验证的代码合成框架，在多种工程力学场景中实现了高成功率的完整物理有效仿真生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专业知识，现有将大语言模型集成到FEA的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限。

Method: (1) 多模态视觉-语言多智能体管道：采用ReAct驱动推理从异构输入（图像+问题描述）中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 优先验证的代码合成框架：集成自调试和回退机制，确保代码可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景中，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，该框架有潜力将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [122] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS Initiative 通过开源基础设施和标准化基准，评估AI辅助建模工具在因果推理、定量纠错等方面的表现，发现当前工具在讨论和基础定性任务上优于因果推理和定量修复，且不同LLM表现存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保AI建模工具负责任且符合伦理，以人类专家为中心而非取代人类，通过建立基准来指导AI工具开发。

Method: 构建开放的数字化和组织化基础设施，设立指导组和技术组，设计自动化测试基准（涵盖因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建议建模步骤和修复等类别），将sd ai引擎与不同LLM耦合进行测试。

Result: AI建模工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，在因果推理和定量错误修复上表现较差；不同LLM在不同引擎类型上表现各异，没有单一LLM占据主导地位，且存在速度与准确性的权衡。

Conclusion: 需要继续纳入关注偏差、替代视角和人本用例的基准，持续推动AI建模工具向负责任和伦理方向发展。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [123] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对约12,000名Bing Copilot用户和WildChat-4.8M数据集的对比分析，发现个体用户与LLM的交互行为具有高度粘性，不同活跃度用户存在显著差异，且WildChat数据集存在严重偏向"高技能用户"的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对用户与LLM交互的描述多为静态快照，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的理解。

Method: 分析约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行对比。

Result: 群体层面存在显著趋势变化，但个体用户行为趋势非常微弱且具有高度粘性；活跃用户对话更成功、任务更复杂且偏向专业；WildChat数据集严重偏向高技能"权力用户"，不代表典型用户交互。

Conclusion: 现存的用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著；WildChat数据集存在重要偏差，使用该数据需谨慎。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [124] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多模型分歧的审计流程，用于检测和引导人类审查LLM在分类公众评论时产生的解释性差异，并证明分歧评估是准确性指标的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估仅依赖对小型验证集的立场准确性，无法检测不同模型对同一公众输入产生实质性不同分类的情况，可能导致政策制定者看到被模型扭曲的记录。

Method: 提出Interpretive Audit Pipeline，通过多模型分歧诊断解释复杂性；分析1,260条USDA公众评论，跨4个LLM比较主题差异；在40条分层子样本上进行两阶段标注研究（独立标注→查看他人标注后修订）。

Result: 模型间主题分歧超过同一模型的提示变动；专家评分标准压制了深层解释分歧但未解决它；人类标注者的修订频繁引入合议输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 分歧评估必须作为准确性指标的补充，用于LLM辅助的解释性编码任务。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [125] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现提示词的语气变化会系统性影响LLM在客观选择题上的准确率，这种影响具有模型依赖性和学科差异性，用户不应假设LLM对语气具有鲁棒可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的使用日益普及，但其性能被观察到随提示风格和语气而变化，本研究旨在系统性地探究语气变化是否以及如何导致LLM在客观选择题上产生不同准确率。

Method: 使用两个数据集（50道基础题×5种语气变体、MMLU 570道题×57个学科×7种语气变体），对ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite四种高效LLM进行实验评估。

Result: 语气效果具有系统性但高度模型依赖：部分模型表现出较小但统计显著的准确率变化，其他模型则在不同语气间出现大幅准确率波动。此外，不同学科对语气的敏感度存在差异。

Conclusion: 提出了一种路由框架来解释语气如何调谐内部推理模式，并警示用户不应假设LLM在部署中对语气具有鲁棒可靠性。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [126] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对72名高等教育从业者的横截面调查，基于DOT框架（设计思维+开放系统理论）探讨了AI在教学中整合的信念、行为与制度条件。探索性因子分析识别出三个信念因子：AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作规划（α=.90）。结果表明从业者认可AI教学支持价值但强调人类监督，实践中迭代提示和内容生成较多但需求评估和反馈循环不足，政策、培训、基础设施等制度障碍普遍存在。研究为DOT框架提供了初步实证支持，并指出了设计导向理论与当前实践之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中AI整合日益重要，但缺乏基于理论的实证研究来系统理解从业者的信念、行为以及制度条件之间的关系。

Method: 横截面调查设计，n=72名高等教育从业者；采用探索性因子分析处理19个信念条目；基于DOT框架（设计思维与开放系统理论的整合）进行理论指导。

Result: 1) 三因子信念结构：AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作规划（α=.90）；2) 从业者肯定AI的教学支持价值但坚持人类监督；3) 实践中迭代提示和内容生成使用较多，需求评估和反馈循环使用较少；4) 制度障碍（政策、培训、基础设施）普遍存在。

Conclusion: DOT框架作为描述性模型获得了初步实证支持；研究揭示了设计导向理论与当前实践之间的差距；提供了初始测量结构，指明了验证性验证和结果导向研究的方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [127] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可微信念对手塑造（D-BOS），通过将观察者的信念视为对手状态并对k步softmax-贝叶斯信念动态进行微分，在隐藏角色游戏中实现对手塑造，实验表明在混合动机场景中优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑造方法通常在对手的参数、策略或价值空间中操作，而隐藏角色游戏中的信念操控技术依赖于硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和），缺乏灵活性。

Method: D-BOS将观察者的信念作为被塑造的对手状态，通过对k步softmax-贝叶斯信念动态进行微分来提供对手塑造信号，并通过聚合多个观察者的推断信念轨迹梯度自然地扩展到多观察者场景。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS优于PPO和BBM，在混合动机设置中增益最大。

Conclusion: 信念状态作为塑造目标允许最优策略从环境奖励结构中自然涌现，无需显式奖励欺骗或合作行为。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [128] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种记忆增强的多智能体架构（HOPE启发下的嵌套学习+连续记忆系统+语义相似性缓存），在310个混合提示上评估了三阶段流水线，通过五个KPI计算的THS显示端到端幻觉减少31.3%-35.9%，语义缓存命中率47.3%减少LLM调用，且高可观测性配置强化而非削弱了缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体流水线中幻觉问题会不受控制地级联传播，成为生产级LLM系统的可靠性障碍，现有方法缺乏可观测性与缓解效果的联合优化，需要在无需模型重训的前提下提升事实可靠性、运行效率和可审计性。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，集成连续记忆系统和语义相似性缓存；构建310个混合提示（217个认知不确定性+93个编造诱导压力测试）；设计三阶段智能体流水线（高随机性前端生成器+两级渐进校正器），通过OFP协议编排；定义五个KPI（FCD/FGR/FDF/ECS/OSR）聚合为THS，在五种权重配置下评估缓解-可观测性权衡。

Result: 端到端THS降低31.3%-35.9%；语义缓存440次命中/930次潜在调用（命中率47.3%），LLM调用降至490次；ExtremeObservability配置获得最负THS（-0.0709），证实高可观测性强化缓解效果。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计能够在无需模型重训的情况下同时提升事实可靠性、运行效率和可审计性，其中高可观测性配置与幻觉缓解并非权衡关系而是相互增强。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [129] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD提出了一种将多步安全推理迁移到连续潜空间的方法，通过阶段式训练课程实现推理时的直接隐状态传播，在10个提示/响应审核设置上，相比Llama Guard 3提升8.24个Macro-F1点，匹配显式推理基线GuardReasoner的性能，同时实现12.9倍加速和22.4倍令牌使用减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的安全护栏虽然优于分类基线，但存在显著的查询延迟和令牌开销，使其难以用于高吞吐量部署。亟需一种既能保持推理优势又能实现高效部署的安全护栏方法。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过阶段式训练课程（stage-wise training curriculum）将多步安全推理过程迁移到连续潜空间，在推理时直接进行隐状态传播（hidden-state propagation），避免显式的推理链生成。

Result: 在8个安全基准的10种提示/响应审核设置上，COLAGUARD相比Llama Guard 3提升8.24个Macro-F1点，与GuardReasoner的Macro-F1性能持平，同时实现12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍令牌使用减少。

Conclusion: 潜空间推理（latent reasoning）为可部署的安全护栏提供了显式理由生成的实用替代方案，能够同时提升安全鲁棒性和推理效率，而非将二者视为相互竞争的目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [130] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个策略无关的执行与测量层，介于调度策略与工业执行环境之间，通过构建决策有效快照、定义标准化执行契约并记录策略意图与执行结果之间的偏离，将执行不确定性转化为可监督的结构化数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动的调度策略在异步、部分可观测的工业环境中，决策状态时间不一致、动作可采纳性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊，限制了可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 提出一个策略无关的执行与测量层，包含三个核心功能：(1) 从异步事件流构建决策有效快照；(2) 定义包含显式动作可采纳性的标准化执行契约；(3) 记录策略意图、事务性结果、物理执行与人工干预之间的分歧作为执行结果。

Result: 离散事件仿真表明：在所有观测滞后区间均展现出分析性收益；将无差异化执行失败转化为带完全归因覆盖的结构化类型化结果；在低观测滞后条件下运营收益最强，可避免的错误能在提交前被阻止。

Conclusion: 该层实现了决策语义与执行行为之间的分离，使部署失配变得可观测且结构可归因，并将执行不确定性转化为用于评估和策略优化的监督数据。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [131] [Trends in AI and Human-AI Interaction in Clinical Trials -- A Hybrid Human-AI Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29096)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析ClinicalTrials.gov注册库中的记录，揭示了AI相关临床试验在术语和地理分布上的时间趋势，并探索了一种混合人机方法用于分析临床试验中的人机交互趋势。研究发现AI相关试验显著增加，中国和美国数量最多，混合人机筛选方法具有潜在可行性，但需要更清晰的试验报告和更精确的交互定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解AI在临床试验中的应用趋势及地理分布，并探索混合人机方法在分析人机交互模式中的可行性。

Method: 从ClinicalTrials.gov检索AI相关临床试验记录，使用GPT-5.5与人工审核相结合的混合工作流对记录进行筛选和分类，分析AI术语的时间趋势、地理分布以及人与AI分类器在识别人机交互方面的一致性。

Result: AI相关试验随时间显著增加，机器学、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT和大型语言模型的引用增长明显；中国和美国占AI相关试验的最大份额，意大利、法国、西班牙、英国和土耳其等国近期增长显著；在100份随机样本中，人与AI分类器在识别非实质性使用AI的研究上一致性良好，但在分类人机交互（尤其是健康专业人员交互模糊或描述不足的情况）时一致性较低。

Conclusion: 混合人机筛选临床试验记录的方法具有潜在可行性，但需要更清晰的试验报告和更精确的人机交互定义以改进该过程。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Consensus: Trace-Level Synthesis in Mixture of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29116)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统LLM多智能体系统通过多数投票压缩推理过程会导致信息丢失。本文发现，直接聚合完整推理轨迹的LLM聚合器能恢复正确答案，即使智能体一致同意时也是如此——这被称为"聚合悖论"。多数投票存在天花板，扰动多样性无法突破（误差相关性相同）；聚合器的增益来自轨迹级互补性，能组装被投票丢弃的少数链中的正确中间步骤。基于此提出自一致混合智能体方法，通过语义保持输入扰动生成轨迹多样性，通过锚定精炼保护多数意见并保证非退化性，始终综合而非门控共识。单一模型通过扰动产生轨迹变异，其表现优于异构模型池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示多数投票在LLM多智能体聚合中的信息损失问题，证明直接聚合完整推理轨迹能获得更好的性能，即使智能体一致同意时也是如此。

Method: 提出Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents (SCMoA)方法：(1) 通过语义保持的输入扰动生成轨迹多样性；(2) 锚定精炼机制保护多数意见，具有可证明的非退化保证；(3) 始终综合所有推理轨迹，而非在共识处门控。

Result: 单一模型通过扰动产生轨迹变异，在结构化推理、博士级科学问题、竞赛数学和竞争编程任务上均优于异构模型池。

Conclusion: 聚合的基本单元应是推理轨迹而非答案。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents solve the same problem, standard practice compresses each agent's reasoning into a majority vote or layered synthesis, treating agreement as the finish line. We show this is unnecessarily lossy: an LLM aggregator that reads complete reasoning traces recovers correct solutions even when agents unanimously agree, with beneficial corrections consistently outweighing harmful ones -- the \emph{aggregation paradox}. Majority voting has a ceiling that perturbation diversity does not raise (error correlations are identical); the aggregator's gain comes from trace-level complementarity, assembling correct intermediate steps from minority chains that voting discards. These findings motivate Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents which generates trace diversity through semantic-preserving input perturbations, safeguards the majority via anchored refinement with provable non-degradation guarantees, and always synthesizes -- never gates on consensus. A single model with perturbation-induced trace variation outperforms heterogeneous model pools across structured reasoning, PhD-level science, competition mathematics, and competitive programming. The unit of aggregation should be the reasoning trace, not the answer.

</details>


### [133] [The Confidence Shortcut: A Reasoning Failure Mode of Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29123)
*Dueun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示置信度导向的解码策略与复杂推理所需的逻辑流轨迹存在根本性不匹配，且置信度对齐的训练会加剧这种错位；而随机掩码虽看似低效，却能稳健保留推理轨迹条件，在复杂推理任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码扩散语言模型采用置信度导向解码作为默认推理策略，但该方法在复杂推理任务（如多位加法）中会先预测局部简单数字而未解决长程依赖，导致高置信度错误。作者旨在揭示这一根本性错位，并证明置信度对齐训练会进一步放大该问题。

Method: 通过多位加法任务进行具体分析，对比随机掩码与置信度对齐训练在不同推理任务（共5种）上的表现，考察解码策略与推理轨迹条件之间的匹配关系。

Result: 置信度导向解码在复杂输入上诱导失败，置信度对齐训练将错误率放大一个数量级；随机掩码则稳健保留推理轨迹条件，在复杂尾部分布上保持低错误率。

Conclusion: 置信度对齐训练与置信度导向解码的组合在复杂推理任务上存在系统性缺陷，随机掩码作为一种替代方案能更好地保留推理所需的逻辑流条件。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) uniquely support any-order generation, with confidence-based decoding currently serving as the de facto standard inference policy. To optimize for this, recent training schemes attempt to align training mask patterns directly with those observed during generation. However, we argue that confidence-based decoding is inherently misaligned with the logical-flow trajectories required for complex reasoning, and that confidence-aligned training actively entrenches this misalignment. We make this concrete using multi-digit addition, where the decoding strategy prematurely predicts locally easy digits before resolving their long-range dependencies, producing high-confidence errors on challenging inputs. While traditional random masking keeps the failure rate low on this challenging tail, confidence-aligned training amplifies the error rate by an order of magnitude. Across five distinct reasoning tasks, this same pattern emerges with task-dependent severity: confidence-based decoding induces failures on highly complex inputs, and confidence-aligned training exacerbates them. In contrast, random masking -- despite its perceived inefficiency -- robustly preserves the reasoning-trajectory conditionals essential for solving the challenging tail.

</details>


### [134] [Better Later Than Sooner: Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge Graph Construction via Ontology-grounded Post-extraction Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29168)
*Lorenzo Loconte,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号框架，通过本体引导的知识图谱构建，结合开放域抽取、嵌入规范化与LLM后校正，在减少token消耗的同时提升知识图谱一致性，并支持SPARQL符号查询以完成复杂问答任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本图的RAG方法缺乏支持符号操作（如聚合、穷举列举）所需的结构，而基于符号知识图谱的方法虽支持SQL式查询，但使用LLM进行KG抽取时存在一致性问题，抽取事实可能违反常识本体约束。

Method: 提出神经符号框架：1) 开放域抽取；2) 嵌入规范化类型和谓词；3) 针对本体违规进行选择性LLM后校正，避免过多LLM调用以降低token开销。

Result: 该方法在减少token使用量的同时，提升了KG的一致性，并保持了下游QA质量；此外，通过SPARQL图模式出现频率的测量，验证了所抽取KG适合符号查询。

Conclusion: 将校正推迟到后抽取阶段，能在不牺牲QA质量的前提下显著降低token消耗并提升KG一致性；本体引导的KG构建为复杂符号查询提供了可靠支撑。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) is a core challenge in AI, particularly for complex queries requiring multi-hop reasoning across documents, or symbolic operations like aggregation or exhaustive listing. Retrieval-augmented generation has become the dominant approach to QA, with recent graph-based variants addressing part of these issues by organizing knowledge to better support compositional questions. However, most textual graph-based RAG methods still lack the structure needed for symbolic operations useful to answer complex questions reliably. This motivates symbolic graph-based approaches, which extract knowledge graphs (KGs) whose relations are logic predicates that enable SQL-like querying. Yet these pipelines typically use LLMs for KG extraction, which can introduce consistency issues, where extracted facts may violate commonsense ontology constraints. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework for ontology-grounded KG construction combining open-domain extraction, embedding-based canonicalization of types and predicates, and targeted LLM-based correction of ontology violations. By deferring corrections to a post-extraction stage, our method avoids repeated LLM calls, substantially reducing token usage while improving KG consistency and preserving downstream QA quality. Finally, we show that the extracted KGs are well suited for symbolic querying by measuring the occurrence of SPARQL graph patterns.

</details>


### [135] [ReasonOps: Operator Segmentation for LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29192)
*Daniel Lee,Owen Queen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReasonOps，一种无监督的、富有表达力的方法，用于为长链思维轨迹（chain-of-thought traces）标注简洁的通用操作符。通过对12种推理大模型在8个推理基准上的44,662条轨迹进行分析，发现存在7种反复出现的推理操作符（如回溯、推断、假设），这些操作符跨模型族和领域普遍存在。基于这些操作符，可以实现模型指纹识别、正确性预测及早期质量估计等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么过于僵化，要么表达能力不足，无法捕捉跨领域和跨模型的思维链结构特征，缺乏一套用于描述长思维链内部结构的词汇体系。

Method: 使用无监督聚类方法，以句子开头的3个token为枢纽（pivot），从大量思维链轨迹中自动发现7种通用推理操作符（discourse-level moves），包括回溯、推断、假设等。采用三位独立LLM评判员对留出样本进行分类验证（准确率70-76%）。

Result: 1) 发现7种跨模型族和基准领域的通用推理操作符；2) 反思性操作符在难题上有助于提升性能，但在简单题上反而有害；3) 操作符序列具有高度模型特异性，可据此识别模型（宏AUC）；4) 基于操作符的特征可显著预测正确性（WP-AUC），在AIME上也有表现；5) 仅用50%的轨迹即可进行早期质量估计（WP-AUC）。

Conclusion: ReasonOps是一种无监督、无需标注的分析方法，能够深入洞察LLM推理轨迹，并在模型识别和正确性预测等下游任务上取得强结果。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces from large reasoning models can span tens of thousands of tokens, yet we lack a vocabulary for describing their internal structure. Previous methods developed to analyze chain-of-thought traces are either too rigid or not expressive enough, failing to capture features across domains and models. To remedy this, we develop ReasonOps, an unsupervised, expressive method for annotating chain-of-thought traces, providing succinct universal operators. Using ReasonOps, we analyze 44,662 traces from 12 thinking LLMs spanning 6 families across 8 reasoning benchmarks and discover that they share a common compositional structure: 7 recurring reasoning operators -- discourse-level moves such as backtracking, inferring, and hypothesizing -- that emerge from unsupervised clustering of sentence-initial 3-token pivots. These operators appear across every model family and benchmark domain, confirmed by three independent LLM judges who classify held-out samples at 70 -76% accuracy. We analyze the structure of operators on easy vs. hard problems, revealing that reflective operators are more helpful on hard problems and harm performance on easy problems. Operator sequences are highly model-identifying: a classifier trained on operator distributions alone recovers the source model with macro-AUC, revealing that each model family has a distinctive reasoning fingerprint. Structural operator features predict within-problem answer correctness well above baselines. Classifiers built on these operators reach WP-AUC and on AIME specifically. ReasonOps further enables early quality estimation well before the trace completes: we predict at WP-AUC for only 50% of the trace. The ReasonOps pipeline is unsupervised and annotation-free, enabling deep insights into LLM reasoning traces as well as strong downstream results on model identification and correctness prediction.

</details>


### [136] [BenchTrace: A Benchmark for Testing Reflection Ability and Controlled Evolution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29225)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Zefan Yu,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BenchTrace基准，用于评估LLM智能体的自我进化能力，包含反思评估和进化评估两个维度，实验发现当前模型在反思诊断上存在瓶颈，且自我进化存在遗忘和负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体的评估存在两个局限：仅衡量任务得分而忽略反思质量，且依赖智能体自身轨迹无法针对特定失败模式进行测试。

Method: 构建包含1,821条标注快照-反思数据集的BenchTrace基准，包含反思评估（通过目标化QA任务探查失败识别能力）和进化评估（在受控模拟中测试失败经验是否转化为规避行为），并提出失败规避率（FAR）指标。

Result: Qwen3-32B和GPT-4.1在反思评估上端到端通过率均低于30%，诊断是主要瓶颈；自我进化方法普遍提升FAR，但存在早期经验遗忘和跨上下文负迁移问题；仅完全正确的反思与高FAR强相关。

Conclusion: BenchTrace揭示了当前自我进化方法的具体局限性，提供了一个受控且模型无关的定向评估框架。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve over time by reflecting on past failures, but existing evaluation is limited in two ways: it measures only task scores, leaving reflection quality unknown, and it relies on agents' own episode runs, offering no mechanism to target specific failure patterns. We present \textbf{BenchTrace}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolution ability in LLM agents. BenchTrace is built on a snapshot-reflection dataset of 1,821 annotated episodes spanning six diverse tasks, and comprises a \textbf{Reflection Evaluation} that probes failure identification through targeted QA tasks, and an \textbf{Evolution Evaluation} that tests whether past failure experience translates into avoidance behavior in a controlled self-evolution simulation. Building on BenchTrace, we propose \textbf{failure avoidance rate (FAR)}, a new evaluation metric measuring the fraction of test cases in which the agent successfully avoids the target failure instance. Experiments with Qwen3-32B and GPT-4.1 reveal that both models fall below a 30\% end-to-end pass rate on reflection evaluation, with diagnosis as the primary bottleneck. Evolution evaluation shows that self-evolution methods generally improve FAR over the non-evolving baseline, but agents forget early lessons as noise episodes accumulate, and agents fail to generalize their reflections beyond the specific context, causing negative transfer across task contexts. Our correlation analysis further reveals that only a fully correct reflection is strongly associated with higher FAR. BenchTrace exposes concrete limits of current self-evolution approaches and provides a controlled, model-agnostic framework for targeted evaluation.

</details>


### [137] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，用于评估数据集与小型学生模型在推理蒸馏中的适配度，实验证明DMC与蒸馏性能强相关，且基于DMC的数据选择（包括动态选择）能有效提升推理蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏中训练数据与学生模型的匹配度对蒸馏效果至关重要，但现有方法缺乏有效评估数据集与学生模型兼容性的量化指标。

Method: 提出DMC指标，联合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生能力三个维度来评估数据集对学生模型的适配性；并设计了基于DMC的静态和动态数据选择策略。

Result: DMC与推理蒸馏性能呈现强相关性；以DMC为标准进行数据选择（包括动态选择）能显著提升多个学生模型和任务上的推理蒸馏效果。

Conclusion: DMC是一个有效的蒸馏数据选择指标，且由于数据集DMC在训练过程中动态变化，动态数据选择策略能进一步优化蒸馏性能。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [138] [Rethinking Literature Search Evaluation: Deep Research Helps, and Human Citation Lists Are Not a Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29234)
*Gaurav Sahu,Laurent Charlin,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从两个互补角度研究大规模文献检索：构建深度检索管线（Deep Research pipeline），通过沿参考文献广度优先扩展大幅提升召回率（RollingEval-Jun25基准上从低于20%提升至80%以上）；同时质疑人类参考文献作为评估基准的有效性，发现仅51%的人类引用被评判为中等相关或更高，而最强AI重排序器达86-88%，且人类引用直接合作者的可能性是AI的2.5倍。结论主张联合报告召回率、主题相关性、多样性和合作距离等多维指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献检索方法性能有限，且人类参考文献作为评估标准可能存在偏差，需要从检索管线改进和评估基准可靠性两个维度进行系统研究。

Method: 构建Deep Research管线，处理完整查询论文并沿参考文献广度优先扩展；使用中立LLM裁判评估人类引用的相关性；基于OpenAlex合作图分析引用偏差。

Result: 深度检索管线在250篇论文基准上将召回率从低于20%提升至80%以上；仅51%人类引用被判定为中等相关以上（AI重排序器86-88%）；人类引用直接合作者的概率是AI的2.5倍。

Conclusion: 不应仅依赖单一维度评估文献检索质量，应联合报告召回率、主题相关性、排序多样性和合作距离等多维指标。

Abstract: We study large-scale literature search from two complementary angles: improving the retrieval pipeline, and stress-testing the human reference list as an evaluation target. First, we implement a Deep Research pipeline that processes the full query paper and expands the retrieved results breadth-first along their bibliographies, and show that it substantially outperforms vanilla API-only search, raising recall on RollingEval-Jun25 (a 250-paper literature-search benchmark) from below 20% to above 80%. Second, we use a neutral LLM-as-a-judge to determine if human references are sound ground truth for the task. We find significant limitations: only 51% of human citations are judged moderately relevant or higher, against 86--88% for the strongest AI-based re-rankers. We study this gap on the OpenAlex co-authorship graph, finding that humans are 2.5x more likely than the best AI re-rankers to cite a direct collaborator. Together, our results argue against single-axis literature-search evaluation: recall, topical-relevance scoring, ranked-list diversity, and a co-authorship-distance diagnostic each measure complementary properties of citation quality and should be reported jointly.

</details>


### [139] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需成绩的透明决策层，通过融合学生学习难度普遍性、自我报告与观察困难的不一致性、未解决教师关切三种信号，对课程主题进行优先级排序，在研究生CS课程中验证了与教师关注和学生自报难度的良好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI增强课堂产生丰富的师生反馈，但这些信号难以转化为及时的教学决策，缺乏在成绩出来前可用的透明决策机制。

Method: 构建可解释决策层，融合三个信号：学习困难普遍性、自我报告与观察困难的不一致、未解决的教师关切；输出带决策记录的主题优先级排序。

Result: top-5重叠3/5，Spearman ρ=0.80(教师)；ρ=0.46(学生)；多信号融合AUC=0.96 vs 单一信号0.91。

Conclusion: 透明协调机制可能在反馈不完整时支持人机协同决策，初步证据表明行为信号与学习相关构念一致。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [140] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang,Shuhang Lin,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DenseSteer框架，通过将小型模型的内部表征向“密集推理”模式（更高信息密度/步，更少推理步数）调制，在不增加训练和推理计算量的情况下提升其数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型（≤3B参数）在多步推理任务上显著落后于大语言模型，需要一种无需额外训练的方法来提升其推理能力。

Method: DenseSteer：一种免训练、推理时干预的框架，通过调制模型内部表征向密集推理模式靠拢，从而增强小模型的推理能力。

Result: 在数学推理基准上取得一致的准确率提升，且不增加token级别的负对数似然。

Conclusion: 密集推理是一种有效的数学问题求解结构方法，能够在无需额外训练或推理代价的前提下提升小模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [141] [Provably Secure Agent Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29251)
*Benlong Wu,Weiming Zhang,Kejiang Chen,Han Fang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑推理根本限制的智能体安全新范式，并引入可执行证明约束动作（ePCA）框架，通过神经符号隔离架构将智能体意图无损形式化为一阶逻辑数学约束，在宏观和微观二维动态对抗系统中实现了零攻击成功率和零误报率，为未来智能系统提供了确定性的底层防御基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖经验语义护栏和概率性大模型裁决器的防御架构在面对复杂语义符号解耦攻击时无法提供确定性的安全下界，存在根本性安全危机。

Method: 提出基于逻辑推理根本限制的安全新范式，并设计了可执行证明约束动作（ePCA）框架，采用神经符号隔离架构，将智能体意图无损形式化为一阶逻辑数学约束后再执行物理操作，放弃对自然语言的语义信任。

Result: 在宏观和微观二维动态对抗系统中，形式验证机制实现了零攻击成功率和零误报率，且计算延迟极低。

Conclusion: 该研究在明确系统假设下提供了条件性的形式化基础，并为构建未来智能系统的底层防御提供了工程范式。

Abstract: As large language models transition from bounded generative engines to agents with expansive execution privileges, AI going out of control precipitates a fundamental crisis in artificial intelligence security. Existing defense architectures heavily rely on empirical semantic guardrails and probabilistic large model adjudicators, mechanisms that fail to provide deterministic security lower bounds when facing complex semantic symbol decoupling attacks. To overcome this empirical semantic guardrail dilemma, this paper proposes a new security paradigm for agents based on the fundamental limitations of logical reasoning. Based on this paradigm, we further introduce an executable Proof-Constrained Action (ePCA) framework with a neural symbolic isolation architecture. This framework abandons semantic trust in natural language, forcing agents to losslessly formalize their intentions into first-order logical mathematical constraints before performing physical operations. Empirical evaluations of macroscopic and microscopic two-dimensional dynamic adversarial systems demonstrate that our formal verification mechanism achieves zero attack success rate and zero false positive rate across the evaluated scenarios, with extremely low computational latency. This research provides a conditional formal foundation under explicit system assumptions and an engineering paradigm for constructing the underlying defense foundation for future intelligent systems.

</details>


### [142] [OpenClawBench: Benchmarking Process-side Anomalies in Real-world Agent Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29253)
*Yibing Liu,Yangze Liu,Xiaolong Yin,Bin Wang,Chong Zhang,Hao Yin,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Outcome-Process Gap 概念，即智能体任务成功但执行过程中存在异常。为此构建 OpenClawBench 数据集（31,264 条轨迹），并提出 FullTax 标注框架（二分类、证据、定位、严重度、可恢复性及5类异常分类）。实验发现 31,135 条通过最终测试的执行中仍有 2,904 条存在过程异常，LoRA 微调 Gemma 3 12B 在干净测试集上 F1=0.729。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务成功可能掩盖执行过程中的异常（如未解决歧义、不安全写入、被忽略错误、弱承诺、能力边界过度承诺），现有评估仅关注最终结果，忽略了这些过程侧故障。

Method: 基于 BFCL 驱动的 OpenClaw 会话构建 31,264 条带注释轨迹；提出 FullTax 框架将轨迹转换为结构化异常监督信号（二分类标签、支持证据、起始/跨度定位、严重性、可恢复性及5类异常分类）。

Result: 在 31,135 条通过任务测试的执行中，2,904 条（约 9.3%）被标记为过程异常；LoRA 微调 Gemma 3 12B 在干净标签测试集上达到二分类 F1=0.729。

Conclusion: 仅基于成功的评估会遗漏大量过程侧故障，OpenClawBench 将真实智能体执行日志转化为可审计、可复用的监督信号，用于研究、诊断和监控运行时智能体可靠性。

Abstract: Task success can hide process anomalies in real-world agent executions. An agent may pass the final task oracle while still accumulating unresolved ambiguity, unsafe external writes, ignored errors, weakly grounded commitments, or capability-boundary overcommitment. We study this mismatch as the Outcome-Process Gap and introduce OpenClawBench, a large-scale dataset for measuring and supervising process-side anomalies in real agent execution processes. OpenClawBench is built from BFCL-driven OpenClaw sessions produced by 6 source models and contains 31,264 annotated trajectories. It aligns task-oracle outcomes with structured process evidence. FullTax converts the aligned trajectories into structured anomaly supervision: binary labels, supporting evidence, onset/span localization, severity, recoverability, and a 5-class anomaly taxonomy. Using OpenClawBench, we make the Outcome-Process Gap measurable. Among 31,135 oracle-passing executions, 2,904 are still labeled process-anomalous under FullTax. These results show that success-only evaluation misses a concrete class of process-side failures in real agent executions. A LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma 3 12B detector trained on the high-confidence FullTax supervised pool reaches binary F1=0.729 on the cleaner-labels held-out test split. Together, OpenClawBench turns real agent execution logs into auditable and reusable supervision for studying, diagnosing, and operationally monitoring runtime agent reliability.

</details>


### [143] [When and How Human Curation Backfires: Preference Alignment under Multi-Model Self-Consuming Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29267)
*Yang Zhang,Xiukun Wei,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模型交互下的自消费训练范式，发现模型间的交叉影响可能削弱甚至逆转单独模型中人类 curation 带来的对齐收益，最终导致长期对齐性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅关注单模型自消费训练中引入人类标注的效果，但实际应用中模型常相互交互并消费彼此的生成数据，多模型场景下的动态行为尚不明确。

Method: 形式化构建了多模型交互自消费训练框架，分析了该动力系统收敛到稳定点的条件，并分别研究了人类 curation 对自身（自影响）及对其他模型（交叉影响）的对齐效果。

Result: 与孤立场景中人类 curation 总是提升对齐不同，多模型交互下交叉影响会削弱甚至反转这一效果，导致长期对齐退化。

Conclusion: 多模型交互会破坏单模型场景中人类 curation 的对齐收益，需要重新审视多模型生态下的数据消费策略。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on synthetic data generated by prior model iterations rather than exclusively on real data. This self-consuming training paradigm can lead to model collapse, divergence, or bias amplification. Recent work (Ferbach et al., 2024) shows that incorporating human curation into the loop can steer a self-consuming model toward human-aligned behavior, but these analyses focus on a single, isolated model that solely consumes its own outputs. In practice, however, models often interact and train on input-output pairs produced by other models. This paper studies self-consuming training in the multi-model regime. We first formalize a framework for interacting self-consuming models and characterize when the resulting dynamical system converges to a stable point. We then examine how human curation of one model affects its own alignment (self-influence) and how such effects propagate to other models (cross-influence). Unlike isolated settings where human curation always enhances model alignment, we show that cross-model interactions can dampen or even invert this effect, ultimately degrading long-term alignment.

</details>


### [144] [CoHyDE: Iterative Co-Training of LLM Rewriter & Dense Encoder for Tool Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29271)
*Vaishali Senthil,Ashutosh Hathidara,Sebastian Schreiber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoHyDE是一种迭代式协同训练方法，将密集编码器和LLM重写器作为共演化系统进行联合训练，在ToolBench约10k工具的API检索任务上，NDCG@5比最强单组件基线提升+2.5pp（标准查询）和+6.3pp（模糊查询），最难模糊查询提升达+8pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种主流方法（对比编码器微调与HyDE式查询扩展）存在互补性失败：微调编码器在查询表面形式匹配目录时表现优异，但不匹配时性能崩溃；零样本HyDE对模糊查询更鲁棒，但会生成脱离目录的假设描述，在查询表述清晰时反而降低检索质量。需要一种能同时兼顾两者的方法。

Method: CoHyDE采用迭代协同训练：编码器使用重写器生成的目录风格假设描述进行InfoNCE重训练；重写器通过DPO针对编码器的检索分数进行偏好对齐；两者均先在工具目录上进行暖启动，然后进入迭代循环。

Result: 在ToolBench约10k工具子集上，三轮CoHyDE迭代后：标准查询NDCG@5提升+2.5pp，保留模糊查询提升+6.3pp，最难模糊查询提升达+8pp。消融实验证实协同训练是关键，单独使用任一组件在模糊查询上最多下降-8pp。

Conclusion: 编码器与重写器的协同演化训练能够同时处理表述清晰和模糊不清的用户查询，弥补了两种传统方法的互补性缺陷，显著提升了大规模API目录检索性能。

Abstract: Tool retrieval over large API catalogs is a core bottleneck for LLM agents: user queries arrive in colloquial, often underspecified language, while the catalog uses technical API vocabulary that no fixed encoder can bridge on its own. The two dominant training approaches, contrastive encoder fine-tuning and HyDE-style query expansion with a frozen LLM, address this problem from opposite ends and fail in complementary directions: the fine-tuned encoder excels when the query's surface form already matches the catalog but collapses when it does not, while zero-shot HyDE is more robust to underspecified queries yet generates catalog-unaware hypothetical descriptions that degrade retrieval when queries are well-formed. We introduce CoHyDE, an iterative procedure that trains the dense encoder and the LLM rewriter as a single co-evolving system: the encoder is retrained with InfoNCE on catalog-style hypothetical descriptions produced by the rewriter, and the rewriter is preference-aligned via DPO against the encoder's retrieval scores, with both sides warm-started on the tool catalog before the loop begins. On a ~10k tool subset of the ToolBench catalog, three rounds of CoHyDE improve over the strongest single-component baseline by +2.5 pp NDCG@5 on standard queries and +6.3 pp on held-out vague queries, with gains as large as +8 pp on the hardest vague tier. Ablations confirm that co-training is the key ingredient: using either component in isolation fails to match CoHyDE on both well-formed and vague queries, with losses of up to -8 pp on vague queries.

</details>


### [145] [Entropy-KL Divergence-based Token Masking: A Novel Approach for Selective Fine-tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29303)
*Qi Liu,Mingdi Sun,Yongyi He,Zhi Zheng,Tong Xu,Yi Zheng,Zhefeng Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EKSFT方法，通过在低数据场景下选择性屏蔽高熵或高KL散度的token，在SFT阶段保持预训练分布完整性，从而提升后续RL探索效率，在数学推理任务上优于标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFT+RL范式中，SFT阶段数据量远少于RL阶段，导致模型对有限样本过拟合，产生分布偏移，阻碍后续RL的有效探索。

Method: 提出EKSFT（Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning），选择性地屏蔽那些表现出高熵或与参考模型高KL散度的token，避免模仿这些高不确定性、导致分布偏移的token，从而在注入任务知识的同时保持预训练分布的完整性。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，EKSFT持续优于标准SFT；从EKSFT模型出发进行RL微调后，后RL性能也持续更好，表明EKSFT改善了RL阶段的探索能力。

Conclusion: 低数据场景下，SFT应优先激活任务相关能力而非记忆特定内容；EKSFT通过选择性屏蔽高不确定性token有效缓解了分布偏移问题，提升了后续RL训练效果。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models. This paradigm provides a cold-start for RL exploration, avoiding the inefficiency of pure RL where on-policy sampling yields insufficient positive samples. However, in practice, existing approaches often use a small amount of data for SFT initialization compared to the RL phase, which can cause the model to fit the limited samples and shift away from its pre-trained distribution. This distribution shift impedes the model's ability to effectively explore during subsequent RL training. To address this challenge, we propose that in low-data regimes, SFT should prioritize activating task-relevant capabilities rather than memorizing specific content. Along this line, we propose EKSFT (Entropy-KL Selective Fine-Tuning), which selectively masks tokens that exhibit either high entropy or high KL divergence from a reference model. By excluding these high-uncertainty, distribution-shifting tokens from imitation, EKSFT injects task-specific knowledge while preserving the integrity of the model's pre-trained distribution. Empirical evaluations on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EKSFT consistently outperforms standard SFT. Further RL fine-tuning from the EKSFT model yields consistently better post-RL performance, indicating improved exploration for the RL stage. Our codes and datasets are available at https://github.com/MINE-USTC/EKSFT.

</details>


### [146] [Rubric-Guided Process Reward for Stepwise Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29310)
*Shenghao Ye,Yu Guo,Zhengheng Li,Shuangwu Chen,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoRo提出基于规则引导的过程奖励框架，通过收集多样化路由轨迹并基于结果、成本和过程质量构建偏好对，训练Rubricor生成查询特定评估规则，Judge在该规则下评分路由轨迹，最终结合过程奖励与结果奖励优化路由策略，在五个推理基准上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐步模型路由方法尽管将路由建模为序列决策过程，但仍仅使用结果奖励（最终答案正确性）监督路由器的训练，未能评估中间路由决策的质量，导致性能弱和泛化能力差。

Method: RoRo包含三阶段：(1) 收集多样化路由轨迹，基于结果、成本和过程质量构建偏好对；(2) 通过交替优化训练Rubricor生成查询特定评估规则，同时训练Judge在该规则下评分路由轨迹；(3) 将过程奖励与结果奖励结合，通过GRPO优化路由策略。

Result: 在五个推理基准上的同族和跨族模型设置下，RoRo一致优于强基线方法，实现更好的准确率与成本权衡。

Conclusion: 引入过程奖励能有效弥补结果奖励在中间路由决策评估上的不足，提升逐步模型路由的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Stepwise model routing improves the efficiency of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) by assigning each reasoning step to a suitable model. Recent methods formulate routing as a sequential decision process and train the router with reinforcement learning. However, although they model routing as a process, they still supervise the router with outcome rewards. Such rewards only reflect final answer correctness and fail to evaluate intermediate routing decisions, which can weaken performance and generalization. To address this gap, we propose RoRo, a rubric-guided process reward framework for stepwise model routing. RoRo first collects diverse routing trajectories and constructs preference pairs based on outcome, cost, and process quality. It then trains a Rubricor to generate a query-specific evaluation rubric and a Judge to score routing trajectories under this rubric through alternating optimization. The resulting process rewards are combined with outcome rewards to optimize the routing policy via GRPO. Experiments on five reasoning benchmarks under both same-family and cross-family settings show that RoRo consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves better accuracy and cost trade-offs.

</details>


### [147] [ConMoE: Expert-Pool Consolidation via Prototype Reassignment for MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29350)
*Yilun Yao,Jiaming Pan,Elsie Dai,Peizhuang Cong,Yaoming Li,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConMoE提出了一种免训练的原型重映射框架，通过选择保留的关键专家作为原型，并将原始专家引用确定性重映射到这些原型上，实现了在不更新权重或微调的情况下压缩MoE语言模型，在多个模型上匹配或超越了剪枝和合并基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE语言模型虽然减少了每词元的计算量，但需要存储和服务所有专家，导致部署时内存开销巨大。现有的后训练压缩方法主要通过剪枝专家或合并权重来缩减成本，但仍有改进空间。

Method: 将后训练MoE压缩建模为专家池整合问题：保留一小部分预训练专家作为可重用的原型，并将每个原始专家引用确定性地重映射到所选原型之一。该方法基于校准的贡献度和可替换性信号选择保留的专家，无需权重更新或后压缩微调。

Result: 在三个预训练MoE模型上的实验表明，ConMoE在多个设置下匹配或超越了强剪枝和合并基线，在deepseek-moe-16b-base上以25%和50%的路由专家缩减均取得了最佳平均分，在Qwen3-30B-A3B和OLMoE-1B-7B-0125上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，确定性重分配是最稳定的组件，而更广泛的跨层共享和后验权重融合依赖于具体模型。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models reduce per-token computation but still require storing and serving all experts, making deployment memory-intensive. Existing post-training compression methods mainly shrink this cost by pruning experts or merging their weights. We formulate post-training MoE compression as expert-pool consolidation: retaining a smaller set of pretrained experts as reusable prototypes and deterministically remapping each original expert reference to one selected prototype. This view separates the reduced expert pool from the reuse structure that represents the original expert slots, and allows prototype sharing within local layer scopes while preserving the original router interface. We propose ConMoE, a train-free prototype remapping framework that selects retained experts using calibration-based contribution and replaceability signals, then redirects original expert calls to the selected prototypes without weight updates or post-compression fine-tuning. Experiments on three pretrained MoE language models show that ConMoE matches or outperforms strong pruning and merging baselines in several settings, achieving the best average score on deepseek-moe-16b-base at both 25% and 50% routed-expert reduction, while remaining competitive on Qwen3-30B-A3B and OLMoE-1B-7B-0125. Ablations indicate that deterministic reassignment is the most stable component, whereas broader cross-layer sharing and post-hoc weight fusion are model-dependent.

</details>


### [148] [PassNet: Scaling Large Language Models for Graph Compiler Pass Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29357)
*Yiqun Liu,Yingsheng Wu,Ruqi Yang,Enrong Zheng,Honglei Qiu,Sijun He,Tai Liang,Jingjing Wu,Yuhan Zhou,Yiwei Zhang,Dongyan Chen,Weihan Yi,Xinqi Li,Siqi Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PassNet，首个面向LLM生成编译器pass的大规模生态系统，包含超过1.8万张计算图的数据集和200个长尾融合任务基准PassBench，实验表明前沿模型在聚合指标上落后TorchInductor 37%，但在单个子图上可实现最高3倍加速，微调小模型可提升2.67倍接近前沿性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量编译器（如TorchInductor）在主流模型上表现优异，但在长尾计算图上存在系统性性能天花板——43%的真实子图在默认编译下出现端到端减速。而现有基于LLM的优化工作仅关注独立内核生成，缺乏将LLM集成到编译器流水线中进行结构化图变换的抽象。

Method: (1) PassNet-Dataset：从10万个真实模型中提取超过1.8万张独特计算图；(2) PassBench：200个精心筛选的长尾可融合任务（共2060个子图），采用错误感知加速分数(ES_t)统一评估正确性、稳定性和性能，并构建分层完整性防御机制防止LLM系统性利用。

Result: 前沿模型在PassBench上聚合落后TorchInductor 37%，但单个子图上LLM可实现最高3倍加速。在约4000条PassNet轨迹上微调小模型获得2.67倍提升，接近前沿模型性能水平。

Conclusion: PassBench具有高度区分性和未饱和特性，LLM编译器优化的瓶颈在于一致性而非能力；PassNet可作为实时训练基础设施推动LLM驱动的编译器优化发展。

Abstract: Modern tensor compilers such as TorchInductor deliver substantial speedups on mainstream models, yet face a systematic performance ceiling on long-tail workloads -- our profiling shows that 43% of real-world subgraphs experience end-to-end slowdowns under default compilation. While LLMs offer a path toward automated optimization, existing efforts focus on standalone kernel generation. We argue that pass generation -- where LLMs author structured graph transformations that integrate directly into compiler pipelines -- is the more appropriate abstraction.
